Files intentionally not created in O-M
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/persist.ts

Main rule
Activity Event remains the source of truth.
A future persist helper may write only one raw activity_events row.
The persist helper must not persist semantic candidates, Value Objects, state facts, deltas, or snapshots.

Draft persist helper boundary after human review
Future file:
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/persist.ts

Future allowed responsibilities:
- Accept ControlledActivityIntakePayload created by payloadBuilder.
- Accept ControlledActivityIntakeIdempotencyResult created by idempotency helper.
- Accept trusted server context produced by future route/auth layer.
- Accept injected DB client from future route layer.
- Build one activity_events insert payload using confirmed columns only.
- Insert at most one raw activity_events row only after a later implementation gate approves DB writes.
- Store idempotency trace in confirmed metadata/json column first.
- Return inserted/existing activity event id and duplicateDetected flag.

Future forbidden responsibilities:
- No AI call.
- No Category Derivation run.
- No categoryCandidates persistence.
- No metricCandidates persistence.
- No stateHookCandidates persistence.
- No Value Object creation.
- No value_object_state_facts writes.
- No activity_state_deltas writes.
- No snapshots writes.
- No runtime route logic.
- No auth decision from client payload.
- No client-provided ownership fields.

Draft row mapping to confirm before actual persist.ts:
- title: confirm exact target column.
- inputText: confirm exact raw input / description / text column.
- source: confirm source/source_type column or metadata-only storage.
- status: confirm accepted raw-intake status value.
- durationMinutes: confirm duration column or metadata-only storage.
- startedAt: confirm start column or metadata-only storage.
- endedAt: confirm end column or metadata-only storage.
- occurredAt: confirm occurred/event timestamp column or metadata-only storage.
- timezone: confirm timezone/time_zone column or metadata-only storage.
- context: confirm metadata/json/details column.
- metadata: confirm metadata/json/details column.
- idempotency trace: store in confirmed metadata/json/details column unless later migration adds dedicated key column.
- trusted actor/user/org/space: use server-owned confirmed columns only.

O-M gate decision:
BLOCK_ACTUAL_PERSIST_TS_CREATION_UNTIL_EXACT_COLUMN_NAMES_ARE_REVIEWED_FROM_THIS_DRAFT
